# Bristlune third-party notices

This index preserves original copyright and license materials by component. Some archives include build-tool, example or other-platform notices; retaining a text does not claim that every file from that archive is part of the Windows runtime. The detailed source/runtime and conditional associations are in [catalog.json](catalog.json).

The corresponding source inputs are available at the [source-input release](https://github.com/hashfunction/brushquay/releases/tag/bristlune-source-inputs-20260912). Source archives, runtime binaries and Microsoft installers are not included in this notice directory.

Microsoft terms apply only to Microsoft Distributable Code. They do not replace Python, Qt or other open-source licenses. The exact Python Windows notice, Runtime end-user terms, Visual Studio downstream distributor reference, and Windows SDK18362 terms are retained separately below.

Qt gray-raster and TSCII attributions remain included with system FreeType and ICU. Optional Qt feature notices are preserved where the original configured result is not established. DrMinGW preserves LGPL2.1, SGI/BSD, zlib and exact demangler compiled-link exceptions; XZ retains its entire required MinGW-w64 Windows notice.

The libvpx1.13.1 notice is explicitly reference material while its producer revision remains unresolved. OpenSSL/Qt/GPL applicability is a separate review; this index does not assert overall compatibility.

## ext_ffmpeg

Primary runtime grants (with exact source and selected-file scope):

- **LGPL-2.1-or-later** — All six exact selected DLLs report LGPL version 2.1 or later in their named exported license functions, established by bounded read-only decoding of their actual x64 return-string bodies. This agrees with the pinned Meson producer configuration: gpl/version3/nonfree disabled. Full original licenses and supplemental per-file notices remain included. Selected files: `bin/libavcodec-61.dll`, `bin/libavfilter-10.dll`, `bin/libavformat-61.dll`, `bin/libavutil-59.dll`, `bin/libswresample-5.dll`, `bin/libswscale-8.dll`. All six exact selected Windows DLLs were independently rehashed and their named exported license/configuration function bodies decoded without execution. Each license function is an x64 RIP-relative LEA into RAX followed by RET and returns LGPL version 2.1 or later; all six configuration functions return the same Meson option string without gpl/version3/nonfree overrides.

Other source-file grants (separate from the primary configured runtime grant):
